		<category name="No Intrusive Scopes v1.2.0">
			<comment>Stops new BL2 weapons from generating with scoped sights. Credit: ancent</comment>
			<comment>Iron sights, red dots, holograms, and no-sight options remain. Existing guns keep their current scopes.</comment>
			<comment>Every original sight entry stays listed so the save sanity check cannot delete existing gear.</comment>
			<comment>Strict inverted stage range is untested in-game. Back up saves first; see the README for the conservative fallback.</comment>
			<comment>Load order: UCP first, then No Intrusive Scopes. Fremington's Edge is exempt.</comment>
